 3413 |     const excluded = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(plot.excluded || {}));
 3414 |     const proc = (s) => {
 3415 |       for (let it = 0; it < 20; it++) {
 3416 |         const pts = s.pts.filter((p) => !((excluded[s.key] || {})[p.instId]) && Number.isFinite(p.x));
 3417 |         if (pts.length < 4) break;
 3418 |         const wpts = pts.map((p) => {
 3419 |           const sd = effSD(s.key, p);
 3420 |           return { x: p.x, y: p.y, w: sd && sd > 0 ? 1 / (sd * sd) : 1, p };
 3421 |         });

 3423 |         if (!fit || !fit.f) break;
 3424 |         let worst = null, maxR = 0;

 3426 |           const sd = effSD(s.key, q.p);
 3427 |           const err = sd && sd > 0 ? sd : 1;

 3439 | 
 3440 |   // ===== Experimental-condition compatibility warnings =====
 3441 |   // When plotting Y as a function of the chosen variable field(s), every OTHER
 3442 |   // experimental condition should be held constant across the instances being
 3443 |   // compared. If it isn't (e.g. plotting vs temperature & pH while concentration
 3444 |   // silently differs between spectra), the fit/plot is scientifically misleading.
 3445 |   const conditionWarnings = useMemo(() => {
 3446 |     const includedInstIds = new Set();
 3447 |     series.forEach((s) => s.pts.forEach((p) => { if (!p.excluded) includedInstIds.add(p.instId); }));
 3448 |     const relevant = d.instances.filter((inst) => used[inst.id] !== false && includedInstIds.has(inst.id));
 3449 |     if (relevant.length < 2) return [];
 3450 |     const issues = [];
 3451 | 
 3452 |     // Variable field(s): flag inconsistent units (e.g. one spectrum's temperature
 3453 |     // in degC, another in K) ΓÇö the x-values would not be directly comparable.
 3454 |     effXFields.forEach((fk) => {
 3455 |       const f = CD_EXPERIMENTAL_FIELDS.find((x) => x.key === fk);
 3456 |       if (!f || !f.unitKey) return;
 3457 |       const withUnit = relevant
 3458 |         .map((inst) => ({ inst, unit: getExpUnit(inst, f) || f.units[0] }))
 3459 |         .filter((v) => getExpValue(v.inst, f.key) !== '');
 3460 |       const unitSet = new Set(withUnit.map((v) => v.unit));
 3461 |       if (unitSet.size > 1) {
 3462 |         const majorityUnit = [...unitSet].sort((a, b) =>
 3463 |           withUnit.filter((v) => v.unit === b).length - withUnit.filter((v) => v.unit === a).length
 3464 |         )[0];
 3465 |         issues.push({
 3466 |           field: f,
 3467 |           isUnitMismatch: true,
 3468 |           refValue: majorityUnit,
 3469 |           offenders: withUnit.filter((v) => v.unit !== majorityUnit).map((v) => ({ instId: v.inst.id, name: v.inst.name, value: v.unit }))
 3470 |         });
 3471 |       }
 3472 |     });
 3473 | 
 3474 |     // Other (non-variable) fields: flag differing values across the compared spectra.
 3475 |     const others = CD_EXPERIMENTAL_FIELDS.filter((f) => !effXFields.includes(f.key));
 3476 |     const norm = (raw) => { const n = parseManual(raw); return n !== null ? Math.round(n * 1e6) / 1e6 : String(raw).trim().toLowerCase(); };
 3477 |     others.forEach((f) => {
 3478 |       const valued = relevant
 3479 |         .map((inst) => ({ inst, raw: getExpValue(inst, f.key) }))
 3480 |         .filter((v) => v.raw !== '' && v.raw !== undefined && v.raw !== null);
 3481 |       if (valued.length < 2) return;
 3482 |       const counts = new Map();
 3483 |       valued.forEach((v) => { const k = norm(v.raw); counts.set(k, (counts.get(k) || 0) + 1); });
 3484 |       if (counts.size <= 1) return;
 3485 |       let refKey = null, refCount = -1;
 3486 |       counts.forEach((cnt, k) => { if (cnt > refCount) { refCount = cnt; refKey = k; } });
 3487 |       const refValue = valued.find((v) => norm(v.raw) === refKey)?.raw;
 3488 |       const offenders = valued.filter((v) => norm(v.raw) !== refKey).map((v) => ({ instId: v.inst.id, name: v.inst.name, value: v.raw }));
 3489 |       if (offenders.length) issues.push({ field: f, refValue, offenders });
 3490 |     });
 3491 |     return issues;
 3492 |   }, [series, effXFields.join('|'), d.instances, used]);

 1773 |     const cleanOutliers = (targetReg = null, targetCmp = null) => {
 1774 |         const nc = cellConfig.map((row) => row.map((c) => ({ ...c })));
 1775 |         let changed = false;
 1776 | 
 1777 |         const processCmp = (reg, cmp) => {
 1778 |             for (let it = 0; it < 20; it++) {
 1779 |                 const pts = [];
 1780 |                 const byC = {};
 1781 | 
 1782 |                 (grid || []).forEach((_, r) =>
 1783 |                     (grid[r] || []).forEach((_, c) => {
 1784 |                         if (r >= activePlateDim.rows || c >= activePlateDim.cols) return;
 1785 |                         const cfg = nc[r][c];
 1786 |                         const role = getRole(r, c);
 1787 | 
 1788 |                         if (
 1789 |                             (cfg.region || 'Primary') === reg &&
 1790 |                             role === cmp.name &&
 1791 |                             !cfg.manualOverride &&
 1792 |                             !cfg.excluded
 1793 |                         ) {
 1794 |                             const n = rawOD(r, c);
 1795 |                             const xc = concOf(r, c, role);
 1796 | 
 1797 |                             if (!isNaN(n) && xc > 0) {
 1798 |                                 const val = viability(n);
 1799 |                                 const k = concKey(xc);
 1800 |                                 if (!byC[k]) byC[k] = [];
 1801 |                                 byC[k].push({
 1802 |                                     r,
 1803 |                                     c,
 1804 |                                     x: xc,
 1805 |                                     y: val,
 1806 |                                     realX: xc,
 1807 |                                     val
 1808 |                                 });
 1809 |                             }
 1810 |                         }
 1811 |                     })
 1812 |                 );
 1813 | 
 1814 |                 Object.values(byC).forEach((cps) => {
 1815 |                     const mean = cps.reduce((s, p) => s + p.y, 0) / cps.length;
 1816 |                     let sd = 0;
 1817 | 
 1818 |                     if (useFixedSD) sd = fSD;
 1819 |                     else if (cps.length > 1) {
 1820 |                         sd = Math.sqrt(cps.reduce((s, p) => s + Math.pow(p.y - mean, 2), 0) / (cps.length - 1));
 1821 |                     }
 1822 | 
 1823 |                     cps.forEach((p) => {
 1824 |                         p.computedSD = sd;
 1825 |                         p.effectiveSD = effectiveSD(cmp.name, p.realX, sd);
 1826 |                         pts.push(p);
 1827 |                     });
 1828 |                 });
 1829 | 
 1830 |                 if (pts.length < 4) break;
 1831 | 
 1832 |                 const ft = fit4PL(pts);
 1833 |                 if (!ft) break;
 1834 | 
 1835 |                 let worst = null;
 1836 |                 let maxRatio = 0;
 1837 | 
 1838 |                 pts.forEach((p) => {
 1839 |                     const pred = 100 / (1 + Math.pow(p.realX / ft.ic50, ft.hill));
 1840 |                     const residual = Math.abs(p.y - pred);
 1841 |                     const err = p.effectiveSD > 0 ? p.effectiveSD : fSD > 0 ? fSD : 1;
 1842 |                     const ratio = residual / err;
 1843 | 
 1844 |                     if (ratio > outlierThresh) {
 1845 |                         if (ratio > maxRatio) {
 1846 |                             maxRatio = ratio;
 1847 |                             worst = p;
 1848 |                         }
 1849 |                     }
 1850 |                 });
 1851 | 
 1852 |                 if (worst) {
 1853 |                     nc[worst.r][worst.c].excluded = true;
 1854 |                     changed = true;
 1855 |                 } else {
 1856 |                     break;
 1857 |                 }
 1858 |             }
 1859 |         };
 1860 | 
 1861 |         if (targetReg && targetCmp) {
 1862 |             processCmp(targetReg, { name: targetCmp });
 1863 |         } else {
 1864 |             Object.entries(processedByRegion).forEach(([reg, comps]) => {
 1865 |                 comps.forEach((cmp) => processCmp(reg, cmp));
 1866 |             });
 1867 |         }
 1868 | 
 1869 |         if (changed) updatePlate({ cellConfig: nc });
 1870 |     };

 1180 |     const concOf = (r, c, role) => {
 1181 |         const rl = role !== undefined ? role : getRole(r, c);
 1182 |         if (!rl || isPlatePreset(rl)) return 0;
 1183 | 
 1184 |         const cfg = cellConfig?.[r]?.[c];
 1185 |         if (cfg && cfg.conc !== null && cfg.conc !== undefined) {
 1186 |             return Number(cfg.conc);
 1187 |         }
 1188 | 
 1189 |         const s = customConc[rl]
 1190 |             ? {
 1191 |                   top: parseFloat(customConc[rl].top) || 0,
 1192 |                   dil: parseFloat(customConc[rl].dil) || 1
 1193 |               }
 1194 |             : { top: tConc, dil: dFact };
 1195 | 
 1196 |         let isHoriz = false;
 1197 |         let step = 0;
 1198 | 
 1199 |         if (rowCompounds[r] === rl) isHoriz = true;
 1200 |         else if (compounds[c] === rl) isHoriz = false;
 1201 |         else if (rowCompounds.includes(rl)) isHoriz = true;
 1202 | 
 1203 |         if (isHoriz) {
 1204 |             for (let i = 0; i < c; i++) {
 1205 |                 if (getRole(r, i) === rl) step++;
 1206 |             }
 1207 |         } else {
 1208 |             for (let i = 0; i < r; i++) {
 1209 |                 if (getRole(i, c) === rl) step++;
 1210 |             }
 1211 |         }
 1212 | 
 1213 |         return s.top / Math.pow(s.dil, step);
 1214 |     };
 1215 | 
 1216 |     const cmpColor = (name, autoIdx) => {
 1217 |         const stored = cmpColors[name];
 1218 |         if (stored && /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(stored)) {
 1219 |             return stored.toLowerCase();
 1220 |         }
 1221 |         return toHex(PALETTE[autoIdx % PALETTE.length]);
 1222 |     };
 1223 | 
 1224 |     const plotCmps = useMemo(() => {
 1225 |         const s = new Set();
 1226 | 
 1227 |         compounds.forEach((c, i) => {
 1228 |             if (
 1229 |                 i < activePlateDim.cols &&
 1230 |                 c &&
 1231 |                 !['cells', 'medium', 'pbs'].includes(String(c).toLowerCase())
 1232 |             ) {
 1233 |                 s.add(c);
 1234 |             }
 1235 |         });
 1236 | 
 1237 |         rowCompounds.forEach((c, i) => {
 1238 |             if (
 1239 |                 i < activePlateDim.rows &&
 1240 |                 c &&
 1241 |                 !['cells', 'medium', 'pbs'].includes(String(c).toLowerCase())
 1242 |             ) {
 1243 |                 s.add(c);
 1244 |             }
 1245 |         });
 1246 | 
 1247 |         (grid || []).forEach((row, r) =>
 1248 |             (row || []).forEach((_, c) => {
 1249 |                 if (r >= activePlateDim.rows || c >= activePlateDim.cols) return;
 1250 |                 const role = getRole(r, c);
 1251 |                 if (role && !['cells', 'medium', 'pbs'].includes(String(role).toLowerCase())) {
 1252 |                     s.add(role);
 1253 |                 }
 1254 |             })
 1255 |         );
 1256 | 
 1257 |         return Array.from(s);
 1258 |     }, [activePlateDim, grid, compounds, rowCompounds]);
 1259 | 
 1260 |     const cmpStats = useMemo(() => {
 1261 |         const stats = {};
 1262 | 
 1263 |         (grid || []).forEach((row, r) =>
 1264 |             (row || []).forEach((_, c) => {
 1265 |                 if (r >= activePlateDim.rows || c >= activePlateDim.cols) return;
 1266 |                 const role = getRole(r, c);
 1267 |                 const conc = concOf(r, c, role);
 1268 |                 if (role && conc > 0) {
 1269 |                     if (!stats[role]) stats[role] = { min: conc, max: conc };
 1270 |                     else {
 1271 |                         stats[role].min = Math.min(stats[role].min, conc);
 1272 |                         stats[role].max = Math.max(stats[role].max, conc);
 1273 |                     }
 1274 |                 }
 1275 |             })
 1276 |         );
 1277 | 
 1278 |         return stats;
 1279 |     }, [activePlateDim, grid]);
 1280 |

 1284 |         if (bgType === 'none') {
 1285 |             setBgOD(0);
 1286 |             return;
 1287 |         }
 1288 |         if (bgType === 'manual') {
 1289 |             setBgOD(bgMan);
 1290 |             return;
 1291 |         }

 1294 |         let n = 0;

 1297 |             (row || []).forEach((_, c) => {
 1298 |                 if (r >= activePlateDim.rows || c >= activePlateDim.cols) return;

 1302 |                     const v = rawOD(r, c);
 1303 |                     if (!isNaN(v)) {
 1304 |                         s += v;
 1305 |                         n++;
 1306 |                     }
 1307 |                 }
 1308 |             })
 1309 |         );

 1321 |             (row || []).forEach((_, c) => {
 1322 |                 if (r >= activePlateDim.rows || c >= activePlateDim.cols) return;

 1330 |                     }

 1338 |     const viability = (raw) => {
 1339 |         const net = raw - bgOD;
 1340 |         const ctrl = cOD - bgOD;
 1341 |         return Math.abs(ctrl) < 1e-6 ? 0 : (net / ctrl) * 100;
 1342 |     };
 1343 | 
 1344 |     const wellColor = (r, c) => {
 1345 |         const role = getRole(r, c);
 1346 |         if (!role) return { bg: '#ffffff', dark: true };
 1347 | 
 1348 |         // Preset labels use the shared very-light palette with dark text.
 1349 |         const preset = platePresetColor(role);
 1350 |         if (preset) return { bg: preset, dark: true };
 1351 | 
 1352 |         const idx = allCmpds.indexOf(role);
 1353 |         const base = cmpColor(role, idx !== -1 ? idx : 0);
 1354 |         const conc = concOf(r, c, role);
 1355 |         if (conc <= 0) return { bg: base, dark: needsDarkText(base) };
 1356 | 
 1357 |         const st = cmpStats[role];
 1358 |         if (!st || st.min === st.max) return { bg: base, dark: needsDarkText(base) };
 1359 | 
 1360 |         const lc = Math.log10(conc);
 1361 |         const lx = Math.log10(st.max);
 1362 |         const ln = Math.log10(st.min);
 1363 |         const frac = lx > ln ? (lc - ln) / (lx - ln) : 0;
 1364 |         const bg =
 1365 |             frac > 0.5 ? darken(base, (frac - 0.5) * 1.0) : lighten(base, (0.5 - frac) * 0.9);
 1366 | 
 1367 |         return { bg, dark: needsDarkText(bg) };
 1368 |     };

 1370 |     const heatColor = (r, c) => {
 1371 |         const cfg = cellConfig[r]?.[c];
 1372 |         if (!cfg || cfg.excluded) return 'transparent';
 1373 |         const v = rawOD(r, c);
 1374 |         if (isNaN(v)) return 'transparent';
 1375 |         const role = getRole(r, c);
 1376 |         if (!role) return 'transparent';
 1377 | 
 1378 |         const rl = String(role).toLowerCase();
 1379 |         if (rl === 'medium') return '#ffe4e6';
 1380 |         if (rl === 'pbs') return '#f3f4f6';
 1381 |         if (rl === 'cells') return '#fef9c3';
 1382 | 
 1383 |         const idx = allCmpds.indexOf(role);
 1384 |         const base = cmpColor(role, idx !== -1 ? idx : 0);
 1385 |         const vb = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, viability(v)));
 1386 |         return lighten(base, 0.25 + (vb / 100) * 0.55);
 1387 |     };

 1426 |     const autoCalcControl = () => {
 1427 |         let sumOD = 0;

 1429 | 
 1430 |         allCmpds.forEach((cmp) => {
 1431 |             if (['cells', 'medium', 'pbs'].includes(String(cmp).toLowerCase())) return;
 1432 |

 1434 | 
 1435 |             (grid || []).forEach((row, r) =>
 1436 |                 (row || []).forEach((_, c) => {

 1438 |                         const conc = concOf(r, c, cmp);

 1440 |                         if (!isNaN(n) && conc > 0) pts.push({ conc, od: n });
 1441 |                     }

 1447 |                 const lowPts = pts.filter((p) => p.conc === minConc);
 1448 |                 lowPts.forEach((p) => {
 1449 |                     sumOD += p.od;
 1450 |                     count++;
 1451 |                 });
 1452 |             }

 1456 |     };

 1458 |     const processedByRegion = useMemo(() => {
 1459 |         const byReg = {};
 1460 | 
 1461 |         if (plateType === '9x9box') return {};
 1462 | 
 1463 |         (grid || []).forEach((row, r) =>
 1464 |             (row || []).forEach((_, c) => {
 1465 |                 if (r >= activePlateDim.rows || c >= activePlateDim.cols) return;
 1466 |                 const reg = cellConfig[r]?.[c]?.region || 'Primary';
 1467 |                 if (!byReg[reg]) byReg[reg] = [];
 1468 |             })
 1469 |         );
 1470 | 
 1471 |         const result = {};
 1472 | 
 1473 |         Object.keys(byReg).forEach((reg) => {
 1474 |             const comps = plotCmps
 1475 |                 .map((name) => {
 1476 |                     const pts = [];
 1477 | 
 1478 |                     (grid || []).forEach((row, r) =>
 1479 |                         (row || []).forEach((_, c) => {
 1480 |                             if (r >= activePlateDim.rows || c >= activePlateDim.cols) return;
 1481 |                             const cfg = cellConfig[r]?.[c];
 1482 |                             if (!cfg) return;
 1483 |                             if ((cfg.region || 'Primary') !== reg || getRole(r, c) !== name) return;
 1484 | 
 1485 |                             const n = rawOD(r, c);
 1486 |                             if (isNaN(n)) return;
 1487 | 
 1488 |                             const xc = concOf(r, c, name);
 1489 |                             if (!(xc > 0)) return;
 1490 | 
 1491 |                             pts.push({
 1492 |                                 realX: xc,
 1493 |                                 logX: Math.log10(xc),
 1494 |                                 val: viability(n),
 1495 |                                 excl: cfg.excluded,
 1496 |                                 r,
 1497 |                                 c
 1498 |                             });
 1499 |                         })
 1500 |                     );
 1501 | 
 1502 |                     const byX = {};
 1503 | 
 1504 |                     pts.forEach((p) => {
 1505 |                         const k = concKey(p.realX);
 1506 |                         if (!byX[k]) byX[k] = { inc: [], excl: [] };
 1507 |                         (p.excl ? byX[k].excl : byX[k].inc).push(p);
 1508 |                     });
 1509 | 
 1510 |                     const vPts = [];
 1511 |                     const ePts = [];
 1512 | 
 1513 |                     Object.keys(byX).forEach((ks) => {
 1514 |                         const g = byX[ks];
 1515 |                         if (g.inc.length === 0 && g.excl.length === 0) return;
 1516 | 
 1517 |                         const rep = g.inc[0] || g.excl[0];
 1518 |                         const xr = rep.realX;
 1519 |                         const xl = rep.logX;
 1520 | 
 1521 |                         if (g.inc.length > 0) {
 1522 |                             const mean = g.inc.reduce((s, p) => s + p.val, 0) / g.inc.length;
 1523 |                             let sd = 0;
 1524 | 
 1525 |                             if (useFixedSD) sd = fSD;
 1526 |                             else if (g.inc.length > 1) {
 1527 |                                 sd = Math.sqrt(
 1528 |                                     g.inc.reduce((s, p) => s + Math.pow(p.val - mean, 2), 0) /
 1529 |                                         (g.inc.length - 1)
 1530 |                                 );
 1531 |                             }
 1532 | 
 1533 |                             vPts.push({
 1534 |                                 x: xl,
 1535 |                                 realX: xr,
 1536 |                                 y: mean,
 1537 |                                 sd: effectiveSD(name, xr, sd),
 1538 |                                 sdRaw: sd,
 1539 |                                 pts: g.inc
 1540 |                             });
 1541 |                         }
 1542 | 
 1543 |                         if (g.excl.length > 0) {
 1544 |                             ePts.push({
 1545 |                                 x: xl,
 1546 |                                 realX: xr,
 1547 |                                 y: g.excl.reduce((s, p) => s + p.val, 0) / g.excl.length,
 1548 |                                 pts: g.excl
 1549 |                             });
 1550 |                         }
 1551 |                     });
 1552 | 
 1553 |                     vPts.sort((a, b) => a.x - b.x);
 1554 |                     ePts.sort((a, b) => a.x - b.x);
 1555 | 
 1556 |                     if (vPts.length === 0 && ePts.length === 0) return null;
 1557 | 
 1558 |                     const color = cmpColor(name, allCmpds.indexOf(name));
 1559 |                     let fit = null;
 1560 | 
 1561 |                     if (fitIC50 && vPts.length >= 3) {
 1562 |                         let sumW = 0;
 1563 |                         const fitData = vPts.map((p) => {
 1564 |                             const w = p.sd > 0 ? 1 / (p.sd * p.sd) : 1;
 1565 |                             sumW += w;
 1566 |                             return {
 1567 |                                 x: p.realX,
 1568 |                                 y: p.y,
 1569 |                                 w,
 1570 |                                 sd: p.sd
 1571 |                             };
 1572 |                         });
 1573 | 
 1574 |                         if (sumW > 0) fitData.forEach((p) => (p.w = (p.w / sumW) * fitData.length));
 1575 |                         fit = fit4PL(fitData);
 1576 |                     }
 1577 | 
 1578 |                     return { name, color, vPts, ePts, fit };
 1579 |                 })
 1580 |                 .filter(Boolean);
 1581 | 
 1582 |             if (comps.length > 0) result[reg] = comps;
 1583 |         });
 1584 | 
 1585 |         return result;
 1586 |     }, [
 1587 |         grid,
 1588 |         cellConfig,
 1589 |         activePlateDim,
 1590 |         fitIC50,

 1678 |     const updateCmp = (c, v) => {

 1690 |             }

 1692 |             let step = 0;

 1773 |     const cleanOutliers = (targetReg = null, targetCmp = null) => {
 1774 |         const nc = cellConfig.map((row) => row.map((c) => ({ ...c })));
 1775 |         let changed = false;
 1776 | 
 1777 |         const processCmp = (reg, cmp) => {
 1778 |             for (let it = 0; it < 20; it++) {
 1779 |                 const pts = [];
 1780 |                 const byC = {};
 1781 | 
 1782 |                 (grid || []).forEach((_, r) =>
 1783 |                     (grid[r] || []).forEach((_, c) => {
 1784 |                         if (r >= activePlateDim.rows || c >= activePlateDim.cols) return;
 1785 |                         const cfg = nc[r][c];
 1786 |                         const role = getRole(r, c);
 1787 | 
 1788 |                         if (
 1789 |                             (cfg.region || 'Primary') === reg &&
 1790 |                             role === cmp.name &&
 1791 |                             !cfg.manualOverride &&
 1792 |                             !cfg.excluded
 1793 |                         ) {
 1794 |                             const n = rawOD(r, c);
 1795 |                             const xc = concOf(r, c, role);
 1796 | 
 1797 |                             if (!isNaN(n) && xc > 0) {
 1798 |                                 const val = viability(n);
 1799 |                                 const k = concKey(xc);
 1800 |                                 if (!byC[k]) byC[k] = [];
 1801 |                                 byC[k].push({
 1802 |                                     r,
 1803 |                                     c,
 1804 |                                     x: xc,
 1805 |                                     y: val,
 1806 |                                     realX: xc,
 1807 |                                     val
 1808 |                                 });
 1809 |                             }
 1810 |                         }
 1811 |                     })
 1812 |                 );
 1813 | 
 1814 |                 Object.values(byC).forEach((cps) => {
 1815 |                     const mean = cps.reduce((s, p) => s + p.y, 0) / cps.length;
 1816 |                     let sd = 0;
 1817 | 
 1818 |                     if (useFixedSD) sd = fSD;
 1819 |                     else if (cps.length > 1) {
 1820 |                         sd = Math.sqrt(cps.reduce((s, p) => s + Math.pow(p.y - mean, 2), 0) / (cps.length - 1));
 1821 |                     }
 1822 | 
 1823 |                     cps.forEach((p) => {
 1824 |                         p.computedSD = sd;
 1825 |                         p.effectiveSD = effectiveSD(cmp.name, p.realX, sd);
 1826 |                         pts.push(p);
 1827 |                     });
 1828 |                 });
 1829 | 
 1830 |                 if (pts.length < 4) break;
 1831 | 
 1832 |                 const ft = fit4PL(pts);
 1833 |                 if (!ft) break;
 1834 | 
 1835 |                 let worst = null;
 1836 |                 let maxRatio = 0;
 1837 | 
 1838 |                 pts.forEach((p) => {
 1839 |                     const pred = 100 / (1 + Math.pow(p.realX / ft.ic50, ft.hill));
 1840 |                     const residual = Math.abs(p.y - pred);
 1841 |                     const err = p.effectiveSD > 0 ? p.effectiveSD : fSD > 0 ? fSD : 1;
 1842 |                     const ratio = residual / err;
 1843 | 
 1844 |                     if (ratio > outlierThresh) {
 1845 |                         if (ratio > maxRatio) {
 1846 |                             maxRatio = ratio;
 1847 |                             worst = p;
 1848 |                         }
 1849 |                     }
 1850 |                 });
 1851 | 
 1852 |                 if (worst) {
 1853 |                     nc[worst.r][worst.c].excluded = true;
 1854 |                     changed = true;
 1855 |                 } else {
 1856 |                     break;
 1857 |                 }
 1858 |             }
 1859 |         };
 1860 | 
 1861 |         if (targetReg && targetCmp) {
 1862 |             processCmp(targetReg, { name: targetCmp });
 1863 |         } else {
 1864 |             Object.entries(processedByRegion).forEach(([reg, comps]) => {
 1865 |                 comps.forEach((cmp) => processCmp(reg, cmp));
 1866 |             });
 1867 |         }
 1868 | 
 1869 |         if (changed) updatePlate({ cellConfig: nc });
 1870 |     };

 1321 |             (row || []).forEach((_, c) => {
 1322 |                 if (r >= activePlateDim.rows || c >= activePlateDim.cols) return;
 1323 |                 const cfg = cellConfig[r]?.[c];
 1324 |                 if (!cfg || cfg.excluded) return;
 1325 |                 if (getRole(r, c) === ctrlType) {
 1326 |                     const v = rawOD(r, c);
 1327 |                     if (!isNaN(v)) {
 1328 |                         s += v;
 1329 |                         n++;
 1330 |                     }
 1331 |                 }
 1332 |             })
 1333 |         );
 1334 | 
 1335 |         if (n > 0) updatePlate({ ctrlODStr: (s / n).toFixed(4) });
 1336 |     }, [grid, cellConfig, ctrlType, gOff, activePlateDim]);
 1337 | 
 1338 |     const viability = (raw) => {
 1339 |         const net = raw - bgOD;
 1340 |         const ctrl = cOD - bgOD;
 1341 |         return Math.abs(ctrl) < 1e-6 ? 0 : (net / ctrl) * 100;
 1342 |     };
 1343 | 
 1344 |     const wellColor = (r, c) => {
 1345 |         const role = getRole(r, c);
 1346 |         if (!role) return { bg: '#ffffff', dark: true };
 1347 | 
 1348 |         // Preset labels use the shared very-light palette with dark text.
 1349 |         const preset = platePresetColor(role);
 1350 |         if (preset) return { bg: preset, dark: true };
 1351 | 
 1352 |         const idx = allCmpds.indexOf(role);
 1353 |         const base = cmpColor(role, idx !== -1 ? idx : 0);
 1354 |         const conc = concOf(r, c, role);
 1355 |         if (conc <= 0) return { bg: base, dark: needsDarkText(base) };
 1356 | 
 1357 |         const st = cmpStats[role];
 1358 |         if (!st || st.min === st.max) return { bg: base, dark: needsDarkText(base) };
 1359 | 
 1360 |         const lc = Math.log10(conc);
 1361 |         const lx = Math.log10(st.max);
 1362 |         const ln = Math.log10(st.min);
 1363 |         const frac = lx > ln ? (lc - ln) / (lx - ln) : 0;
 1364 |         const bg =
 1365 |             frac > 0.5 ? darken(base, (frac - 0.5) * 1.0) : lighten(base, (0.5 - frac) * 0.9);
 1366 | 
 1367 |         return { bg, dark: needsDarkText(bg) };
 1368 |     };
 1369 | 
 1370 |     const heatColor = (r, c) => {
 1371 |         const cfg = cellConfig[r]?.[c];
 1372 |         if (!cfg || cfg.excluded) return 'transparent';
 1373 |         const v = rawOD(r, c);
 1374 |         if (isNaN(v)) return 'transparent';
 1375 |         const role = getRole(r, c);
 1376 |         if (!role) return 'transparent';
 1377 | 
 1378 |         const rl = String(role).toLowerCase();
 1379 |         if (rl === 'medium') return '#ffe4e6';
 1380 |         if (rl === 'pbs') return '#f3f4f6';
 1381 |         if (rl === 'cells') return '#fef9c3';
 1382 | 
 1383 |         const idx = allCmpds.indexOf(role);
 1384 |         const base = cmpColor(role, idx !== -1 ? idx : 0);
 1385 |         const vb = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, viability(v)));
 1386 |         return lighten(base, 0.25 + (vb / 100) * 0.55);
 1387 |     };
 1388 | 
 1389 |     const displayVal = (r, c) => {
 1390 |         const n = rawOD(r, c);
 1391 |         if (isNaN(n)) return grid?.[r]?.[c] === '' ? '' : grid?.[r]?.[c];
 1392 |         return showViab ? viability(n).toFixed(1) + '%' : n.toFixed(3);
 1393 |     };
 1394 | 
 1395 |     const getManual = (name, realX) =>
 1396 |         manualErrors[name] ? manualErrors[name][concKey(realX)] : undefined;
 1397 | 
 1398 |     const hasManual = (name, realX) => typeof getManual(name, realX) === 'number';
 1399 | 
 1400 |     const effectiveSD = (name, realX, computedSD) => {
 1401 |         const m = getManual(name, realX);
 1402 |         return typeof m === 'number' ? m : computedSD;
 1403 |     };
 1404 | 
 1405 |     const storeManual = (name, realX, val) => {

 1408 |         updatePlate({ manualErrors: { ...manualErrors, [name]: inner } });
 1409 |     };
 1410 | 
 1411 |     const clearManual = (name, realX) => {

 1413 |         delete inner[concKey(realX)];
 1414 |         const next = { ...manualErrors };
 1415 |         if (Object.keys(inner).length === 0) delete next[name];
 1416 |         else next[name] = inner;
 1417 |         updatePlate({ manualErrors: next });
 1418 |     };
 1419 | 
 1420 |     const clearAllManual = (name) => {
 1421 |         const next = { ...manualErrors };

 1423 |         updatePlate({ manualErrors: next });
 1424 |     };
 1425 | 
 1426 |     const autoCalcControl = () => {
 1427 |         let sumOD = 0;
 1428 |         let count = 0;
 1429 | 
 1430 |         allCmpds.forEach((cmp) => {
 1431 |             if (['cells', 'medium', 'pbs'].includes(String(cmp).toLowerCase())) return;
 1432 | 
 1433 |             const pts = [];
 1434 | 
 1435 |             (grid || []).forEach((row, r) =>
 1436 |                 (row || []).forEach((_, c) => {
 1437 |                     if (getRole(r, c) === cmp && !cellConfig[r]?.[c]?.excluded) {
 1438 |                         const conc = concOf(r, c, cmp);
 1439 |                         const n = rawOD(r, c);
 1440 |                         if (!isNaN(n) && conc > 0) pts.push({ conc, od: n });
 1441 |                     }
 1442 |                 })
 1443 |             );
 1444 | 
 1445 |             if (pts.length > 0) {
 1446 |                 const minConc = Math.min(...pts.map((p) => p.conc));
 1447 |                 const lowPts = pts.filter((p) => p.conc === minConc);
 1448 |                 lowPts.forEach((p) => {
 1449 |                     sumOD += p.od;
 1450 |                     count++;
 1451 |                 });
 1452 |             }
 1453 |         });
 1454 | 
 1455 |         if (count > 0) updatePlate({ ctrlType: 'none', ctrlODStr: (sumOD / count).toFixed(4) });
 1456 |     };
 1457 | 
 1458 |     const processedByRegion = useMemo(() => {
 1459 |         const byReg = {};
 1460 | 
 1461 |         if (plateType === '9x9box') return {};
 1462 | 
 1463 |         (grid || []).forEach((row, r) =>
 1464 |             (row || []).forEach((_, c) => {
 1465 |                 if (r >= activePlateDim.rows || c >= activePlateDim.cols) return;
 1466 |                 const reg = cellConfig[r]?.[c]?.region || 'Primary';
 1467 |                 if (!byReg[reg]) byReg[reg] = [];
 1468 |             })
 1469 |         );
 1470 | 
 1471 |         const result = {};
 1472 | 
 1473 |         Object.keys(byReg).forEach((reg) => {
 1474 |             const comps = plotCmps
 1475 |                 .map((name) => {
 1476 |                     const pts = [];
 1477 | 
 1478 |                     (grid || []).forEach((row, r) =>
 1479 |                         (row || []).forEach((_, c) => {
 1480 |                             if (r >= activePlateDim.rows || c >= activePlateDim.cols) return;
 1481 |                             const cfg = cellConfig[r]?.[c];
 1482 |                             if (!cfg) return;
 1483 |                             if ((cfg.region || 'Primary') !== reg || getRole(r, c) !== name) return;
 1484 | 
 1485 |                             const n = rawOD(r, c);
 1486 |                             if (isNaN(n)) return;
 1487 | 
 1488 |                             const xc = concOf(r, c, name);
 1489 |                             if (!(xc > 0)) return;
 1490 | 
 1491 |                             pts.push({
 1492 |                                 realX: xc,
 1493 |                                 logX: Math.log10(xc),
 1494 |                                 val: viability(n),
 1495 |                                 excl: cfg.excluded,
 1496 |                                 r,
 1497 |                                 c
 1498 |                             });
 1499 |                         })
 1500 |                     );
 1501 | 
 1502 |                     const byX = {};
 1503 | 
 1504 |                     pts.forEach((p) => {
 1505 |                         const k = concKey(p.realX);
 1506 |                         if (!byX[k]) byX[k] = { inc: [], excl: [] };
 1507 |                         (p.excl ? byX[k].excl : byX[k].inc).push(p);
 1508 |                     });
 1509 | 
 1510 |                     const vPts = [];
 1511 |                     const ePts = [];
 1512 | 
 1513 |                     Object.keys(byX).forEach((ks) => {
 1514 |                         const g = byX[ks];
 1515 |                         if (g.inc.length === 0 && g.excl.length === 0) return;
 1516 | 
 1517 |                         const rep = g.inc[0] || g.excl[0];
 1518 |                         const xr = rep.realX;
 1519 |                         const xl = rep.logX;
 1520 | 
 1521 |                         if (g.inc.length > 0) {
 1522 |                             const mean = g.inc.reduce((s, p) => s + p.val, 0) / g.inc.length;
 1523 |                             let sd = 0;
 1524 | 
 1525 |                             if (useFixedSD) sd = fSD;
 1526 |                             else if (g.inc.length > 1) {
 1527 |                                 sd = Math.sqrt(
 1528 |                                     g.inc.reduce((s, p) => s + Math.pow(p.val - mean, 2), 0) /
 1529 |                                         (g.inc.length - 1)
 1530 |                                 );
 1531 |                             }
 1532 | 
 1533 |                             vPts.push({
 1534 |                                 x: xl,
 1535 |                                 realX: xr,
 1536 |                                 y: mean,
 1537 |                                 sd: effectiveSD(name, xr, sd),
 1538 |                                 sdRaw: sd,
 1539 |                                 pts: g.inc
 1540 |                             });
 1541 |                         }
 1542 | 
 1543 |                         if (g.excl.length > 0) {
 1544 |                             ePts.push({
 1545 |                                 x: xl,
 1546 |                                 realX: xr,
 1547 |                                 y: g.excl.reduce((s, p) => s + p.val, 0) / g.excl.length,
 1548 |                                 pts: g.excl
 1549 |                             });
 1550 |                         }
 1551 |                     });
 1552 | 
 1553 |                     vPts.sort((a, b) => a.x - b.x);
 1554 |                     ePts.sort((a, b) => a.x - b.x);
 1555 | 
 1556 |                     if (vPts.length === 0 && ePts.length === 0) return null;
 1557 | 
 1558 |                     const color = cmpColor(name, allCmpds.indexOf(name));
 1559 |                     let fit = null;
 1560 | 
 1561 |                     if (fitIC50 && vPts.length >= 3) {
 1562 |                         let sumW = 0;
 1563 |                         const fitData = vPts.map((p) => {
 1564 |                             const w = p.sd > 0 ? 1 / (p.sd * p.sd) : 1;
 1565 |                             sumW += w;
 1566 |                             return {
 1567 |                                 x: p.realX,
 1568 |                                 y: p.y,
 1569 |                                 w,
 1570 |                                 sd: p.sd
 1571 |                             };
 1572 |                         });
 1573 | 
 1574 |                         if (sumW > 0) fitData.forEach((p) => (p.w = (p.w / sumW) * fitData.length));
 1575 |                         fit = fit4PL(fitData);
 1576 |                     }
 1577 | 
 1578 |                     return { name, color, vPts, ePts, fit };
 1579 |                 })
 1580 |                 .filter(Boolean);
 1581 | 
 1582 |             if (comps.length > 0) result[reg] = comps;
 1583 |         });
 1584 | 
 1585 |         return result;
 1586 |     }, [
 1587 |         grid,
 1588 |         cellConfig,
 1589 |         activePlateDim,
 1590 |         fitIC50,
 1591 |         useFixedSD,
 1592 |         fSD,
 1593 |         plotCmps,
 1594 |         allCmpds,
 1595 |         plateType
 1596 |     ]);
 1597 | 
 1598 |     const model = useMemo(
 1599 |         () => ({
 1600 |             processedByRegion,
 1601 |             plotCmps,
 1602 |             bgOD,
 1603 |             eScale,
 1604 |             activePlateDim,
 1605 |             unit
 1606 |         }),

 1608 |     );
 1609 | 
 1610 |     useEffect(() => {

 1612 |     }, [model, plateModelRef]);
 1613 | 
 1614 |     const autoTouchSD = (region, name) => {
 1615 |         const compData = (processedByRegion[region] || []).find((c) => c.name === name);
 1616 |         if (!compData || !compData.fit) return;
 1617 | 
 1618 |         let maxRes = 0;
 1619 | 
 1620 |         compData.vPts.forEach((pt) => {
 1621 |             const yFit = 100 / (1 + Math.pow(pt.realX / compData.fit.ic50, compData.fit.hill));
 1622 |             const res = Math.abs(pt.y - yFit);
 1623 |             if (res > maxRes) maxRes = res;
 1624 |         });
 1625 | 
 1626 |         const newSD = Math.ceil((maxRes * 1.02 + 0.01) * 100) / 100;
 1627 |         const inner = { ...(manualErrors[name] || {}) };
 1628 | 
 1629 |         compData.vPts.forEach((pt) => {
 1630 |             inner[concKey(pt.realX)] = newSD;
 1631 |         });
 1632 |

 1634 |     };
 1635 | 
 1636 |     const autoTouchAll = () => {
 1637 |         let updates = {};
 1638 | 
 1639 |         Object.entries(processedByRegion).forEach(([, comps]) => {
 1640 |             comps.forEach((c) => {
 1641 |                 if (!c.fit) return;
 1642 | 
 1643 |                 let maxRes = 0;
 1644 | 
 1645 |                 c.vPts.forEach((pt) => {
 1646 |                     const yFit = 100 / (1 + Math.pow(pt.realX / c.fit.ic50, c.fit.hill));
 1647 |                     const res = Math.abs(pt.y - yFit);
 1648 |                     if (res > maxRes) maxRes = res;
 1649 |                 });
 1650 | 
 1651 |                 const newSD = Math.ceil((maxRes * 1.02 + 0.01) * 100) / 100;
 1652 | 
 1653 |                 if (!updates[c.name]) updates[c.name] = {};
 1654 | 
 1655 |                 c.vPts.forEach((pt) => {
 1656 |                     updates[c.name][concKey(pt.realX)] = newSD;
 1657 |                 });
 1658 |             });
 1659 |         });
 1660 | 
 1661 |         const next = { ...manualErrors };
 1662 | 
 1663 |         Object.keys(updates).forEach((cmp) => {
 1664 |             next[cmp] = { ...(next[cmp] || {}), ...updates[cmp] };
 1665 |         });
 1666 |

 1668 |     };
 1669 | 
 1670 |     const revertNormalSD = () => updatePlate({ manualErrors: {}, useFixedSD: false });
 1671 | 
 1672 |     const updateCell = (r, c, v) => {
 1673 |         const ng = grid.map((row) => [...row]);
 1674 |         ng[r][c] = v;
 1675 |         updatePlate({ grid: ng });
 1676 |     };
 1677 | 
 1678 |     const updateCmp = (c, v) => {
 1679 |         const role = v;
 1680 |         const nc = cellConfig.map((row) => row.map((cell) => ({ ...cell })));
 1681 |         const oldRole = compounds[c];
 1682 |         const isCtrl = role && ['cells', 'medium', 'pbs'].includes(role.toLowerCase());
 1683 | 
 1684 |         if (!role) {
 1685 |             for (let r = 0; r < activePlateDim.rows; r++) {
 1686 |                 if (nc[r][c].role === oldRole) {
 1687 |                     nc[r][c].role = null;
 1688 |                     nc[r][c].conc = null;
 1689 |                 }
 1690 |             }
 1691 |         } else {
 1692 |             let step = 0;
 1693 |             for (let r = 0; r < activePlateDim.rows; r++) {
 1694 |                 if (!nc[r][c].role || nc[r][c].role === oldRole) {
 1695 |                     nc[r][c].role = role;
 1696 |                     if (isCtrl) nc[r][c].conc = 0;
 1697 |                     else {
 1698 |                         const s = customConc[role] || { top: tConc, dil: dFact };
 1699 |                         nc[r][c].conc = s.top / Math.pow(s.dil, step);
 1700 |                     }
 1701 |                     step++;
 1702 |                 }
 1703 |             }
 1704 |         }
 1705 | 
 1706 |         const ncc = [...compounds];
 1707 |         ncc[c] = role;
 1708 |         updatePlate({ cellConfig: nc, compounds: ncc });
 1709 | 
 1710 |         const t = (v || '').trim();
 1711 |         if (t && !allCmpds.includes(t) && setCustomCmpds) {
 1712 |             setCustomCmpds((p) => [...(Array.isArray(p) ? p : []), t]);
 1713 |         }
 1714 |     };
 1715 | 
 1716 |     const updateRowCmp = (r, v) => {
 1717 |         const role = v;
 1718 |         const nc = cellConfig.map((row) => row.map((cell) => ({ ...cell })));
 1719 |         const oldRole = rowCompounds[r];
 1720 |         const isCtrl = role && ['cells', 'medium', 'pbs'].includes(role.toLowerCase());
 1721 | 
 1722 |         if (!role) {
 1723 |             for (let c = 0; c < activePlateDim.cols; c++) {
 1724 |                 if (nc[r][c].role === oldRole) {
 1725 |                     nc[r][c].role = null;
 1726 |                     nc[r][c].conc = null;
 1727 |                 }
 1728 |             }
 1729 |         } else {
 1730 |             let step = 0;
 1731 |             for (let c = 0; c < activePlateDim.cols; c++) {
 1732 |                 if (!nc[r][c].role || nc[r][c].role === oldRole) {
 1733 |                     nc[r][c].role = role;
 1734 |                     if (isCtrl) nc[r][c].conc = 0;
 1735 |                     else {
 1736 |                         const s = customConc[role] || { top: tConc, dil: dFact };
 1737 |                         nc[r][c].conc = s.top / Math.pow(s.dil, step);
 1738 |                     }
 1739 |                     step++;
 1740 |                 }
 1741 |             }
 1742 |         }
 1743 | 
 1744 |         const nrc = [...rowCompounds];
 1745 |         nrc[r] = role;
 1746 |         updatePlate({ cellConfig: nc, rowCompounds: nrc });
 1747 | 
 1748 |         const t = (v || '').trim();
 1749 |         if (t && !allCmpds.includes(t) && setCustomCmpds) {
 1750 |             setCustomCmpds((p) => [...(Array.isArray(p) ? p : []), t]);
 1751 |         }
 1752 |     };
 1753 | 
 1754 |     const updateCellCfg = (r, c, upd) => {
 1755 |         const nc = cellConfig.map((row) => row.map((cell) => ({ ...cell })));
 1756 |         nc[r][c] = { ...nc[r][c], ...upd };
 1757 |         updatePlate({ cellConfig: nc });
 1758 |     };
 1759 | 
 1760 |     // Fill every well of the current selection with a preset label (cells /
 1761 |     // PBS / DMSO / Medium / empty) and NO concentration.
 1762 |     const patchSelectionRole = (label) => {
 1763 |         if (!activeSel) return;
 1764 |         const nc = cellConfig.map((row) => row.map((cell) => ({ ...cell })));
 1765 |         for (let r = activeSel.minR; r <= activeSel.maxR; r++) {
 1766 |             for (let c = activeSel.minC; c <= activeSel.maxC; c++) {
 1767 |                 nc[r][c] = { ...nc[r][c], role: label, conc: null };
 1768 |             }
 1769 |         }

 1771 |     };
 1772 | 
 1773 |     const cleanOutliers = (targetReg = null, targetCmp = null) => {
 1774 |         const nc = cellConfig.map((row) => row.map((c) => ({ ...c })));
 1775 |         let changed = false;
 1776 | 
 1777 |         const processCmp = (reg, cmp) => {
 1778 |             for (let it = 0; it < 20; it++) {
 1779 |                 const pts = [];
 1780 |                 const byC = {};
 1781 | 
 1782 |                 (grid || []).forEach((_, r) =>
 1783 |                     (grid[r] || []).forEach((_, c) => {
 1784 |                         if (r >= activePlateDim.rows || c >= activePlateDim.cols) return;
 1785 |                         const cfg = nc[r][c];
 1786 |                         const role = getRole(r, c);
 1787 | 
 1788 |                         if (
 1789 |                             (cfg.region || 'Primary') === reg &&
 1790 |                             role === cmp.name &&
 1791 |                             !cfg.manualOverride &&
 1792 |                             !cfg.excluded
 1793 |                         ) {
 1794 |                             const n = rawOD(r, c);
 1795 |                             const xc = concOf(r, c, role);
 1796 | 
 1797 |                             if (!isNaN(n) && xc > 0) {
 1798 |                                 const val = viability(n);
 1799 |                                 const k = concKey(xc);
 1800 |                                 if (!byC[k]) byC[k] = [];
 1801 |                                 byC[k].push({
 1802 |                                     r,
 1803 |                                     c,
 1804 |                                     x: xc,
 1805 |                                     y: val,
 1806 |                                     realX: xc,
 1807 |                                     val
 1808 |                                 });
 1809 |                             }
 1810 |                         }
 1811 |                     })
 1812 |                 );
 1813 | 
 1814 |                 Object.values(byC).forEach((cps) => {
 1815 |                     const mean = cps.reduce((s, p) => s + p.y, 0) / cps.length;
 1816 |                     let sd = 0;
 1817 | 
 1818 |                     if (useFixedSD) sd = fSD;
 1819 |                     else if (cps.length > 1) {
 1820 |                         sd = Math.sqrt(cps.reduce((s, p) => s + Math.pow(p.y - mean, 2), 0) / (cps.length - 1));
 1821 |                     }
 1822 | 
 1823 |                     cps.forEach((p) => {
 1824 |                         p.computedSD = sd;
 1825 |                         p.effectiveSD = effectiveSD(cmp.name, p.realX, sd);
 1826 |                         pts.push(p);
 1827 |                     });
 1828 |                 });
 1829 | 
 1830 |                 if (pts.length < 4) break;
 1831 | 
 1832 |                 const ft = fit4PL(pts);
 1833 |                 if (!ft) break;
 1834 | 
 1835 |                 let worst = null;
 1836 |                 let maxRatio = 0;
 1837 | 
 1838 |                 pts.forEach((p) => {
 1839 |                     const pred = 100 / (1 + Math.pow(p.realX / ft.ic50, ft.hill));
 1840 |                     const residual = Math.abs(p.y - pred);
 1841 |                     const err = p.effectiveSD > 0 ? p.effectiveSD : fSD > 0 ? fSD : 1;
 1842 |                     const ratio = residual / err;
 1843 | 
 1844 |                     if (ratio > outlierThresh) {
 1845 |                         if (ratio > maxRatio) {
 1846 |                             maxRatio = ratio;
 1847 |                             worst = p;
 1848 |                         }
 1849 |                     }
 1850 |                 });
 1851 | 
 1852 |                 if (worst) {
 1853 |                     nc[worst.r][worst.c].excluded = true;
 1854 |                     changed = true;
 1855 |                 } else {
 1856 |                     break;
 1857 |                 }
 1858 |             }
 1859 |         };
 1860 | 
 1861 |         if (targetReg && targetCmp) {
 1862 |             processCmp(targetReg, { name: targetCmp });
 1863 |         } else {
 1864 |             Object.entries(processedByRegion).forEach(([reg, comps]) => {
 1865 |                 comps.forEach((cmp) => processCmp(reg, cmp));
 1866 |             });
 1867 |         }
 1868 | 
 1869 |         if (changed) updatePlate({ cellConfig: nc });
 1870 |     };
 1871 | 
 1872 |     const exportXLS = () => {
 1873 |         try {
 1874 |             const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
 1875 | 
 1876 |             const rawAoa = [['', ...COLS]];
 1877 |             ROWS.forEach((rl, r) => {
 1878 |                 rawAoa.push([
 1879 |                     rl,
 1880 |                     ...COLS.map((_, c) => {
 1881 |                         const v = grid?.[r]?.[c];
 1882 |                         return v === '' ? '' : Number(v);
 1883 |                     })
 1884 |                 ]);
 1885 |             });
 1886 |             XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet(rawAoa), 'Raw OD');
 1887 | 
 1888 |             const viabAoa = [['', ...COLS]];
 1889 |             ROWS.forEach((rl, r) => {
 1890 |                 viabAoa.push([
 1891 |                     rl,
 1892 |                     ...COLS.map((_, c) => {
 1893 |                         const n = rawOD(r, c);
 1894 |                         if (isNaN(n) || cellConfig[r]?.[c]?.excluded) return '';

 2738 |   const autoTouch = (s) => {
 2739 |     const fit = fits[s.key] || fitOf(s);
 2740 |     if (!fit || !fit.f) return;
 2741 |     const inner = { ...(plot.manualSD[s.key] || {}) };
 2742 |     includedPts(s).forEach((p) => {
 2743 |       if (!Number.isFinite(p.x)) return;
 2744 |       inner[p.instId] = Math.ceil((Math.abs(p.y - fit.f(p.x)) * 1.02 + 0.01) * 100) / 100;
 2745 |     });
 2746 |     set({ manualSD: { ...plot.manualSD, [s.key]: inner } });
 2747 |   };
 2748 |   const resetSD = (sKey) => { const next = { ...plot.manualSD }; delete next[sKey]; set({ manualSD: next }); };
 2749 |   const cleanOutliers = (target) => {
 2750 |     const thresh = parseManual(plot.outlierThreshStr) || 2.5;
 2751 |     const excluded = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(plot.excluded || {}));
 2752 |     const proc = (s) => {
 2753 |       for (let it = 0; it < 20; it++) {
 2754 |         const pts = s.pts.filter((p) => !((excluded[s.key] || {})[p.instId]) && Number.isFinite(p.x));
 2755 |         if (pts.length < 4) break;
 2756 |         const wpts = pts.map((p) => {
 2757 |           const sd = effSD(s.key, p);
 2758 |           return { x: p.x, y: p.y, w: sd && sd > 0 ? 1 / (sd * sd) : 1, p };
 2759 |         });

 2761 |         if (!fit || !fit.f) break;
 2762 |         let worst = null, maxR = 0;

 2764 |           const sd = effSD(s.key, q.p);
 2765 |           const err = sd && sd > 0 ? sd : 1;
 2766 |           const ratio = Math.abs(q.p.y - fit.f(q.p.x)) / err;
 2767 |           if (ratio > thresh && ratio > maxR) { maxR = ratio; worst = q.p; }
 2768 |         });

 2778 |   // ===== Experimental-condition compatibility warnings =====
 2779 |   // When plotting Y as a function of the chosen variable field(s), every OTHER
 2780 |   // experimental condition should be held constant across the instances being
 2781 |   // compared. If it isn't (e.g. plotting vs temperature & pH while concentration
 2782 |   // silently differs between spectra), the fit/plot is scientifically misleading.
 2783 |   const conditionWarnings = useMemo(() => {
 2784 |     const includedInstIds = new Set();
 2785 |     series.forEach((s) => s.pts.forEach((p) => { if (!p.excluded) includedInstIds.add(p.instId); }));
 2786 |     const relevant = d.instances.filter((inst) => used[inst.id] !== false && includedInstIds.has(inst.id));
 2787 |     if (relevant.length < 2) return [];
 2788 |     const issues = [];
 2789 | 
 2790 |     // Variable field(s): flag inconsistent units (e.g. one spectrum's temperature
 2791 |     // in degC, another in K) ΓÇö the x-values would not be directly comparable.
 2792 |     effXFields.forEach((fk) => {
 2793 |       const f = SSNMR_EXPERIMENTAL_FIELDS.find((x) => x.key === fk);
 2794 |       if (!f || !f.unitKey) return;
 2795 |       const withUnit = relevant
 2796 |         .map((inst) => ({ inst, unit: getExpUnit(inst, f) || f.units[0] }))
 2797 |         .filter((v) => getExpValue(v.inst, f.key) !== '');
 2798 |       const unitSet = new Set(withUnit.map((v) => v.unit));
 2799 |       if (unitSet.size > 1) {
 2800 |         const majorityUnit = [...unitSet].sort((a, b) =>
 2801 |           withUnit.filter((v) => v.unit === b).length - withUnit.filter((v) => v.unit === a).length
 2802 |         )[0];
 2803 |         issues.push({
 2804 |           field: f,
 2805 |           isUnitMismatch: true,
 2806 |           refValue: majorityUnit,
 2807 |           offenders: withUnit.filter((v) => v.unit !== majorityUnit).map((v) => ({ instId: v.inst.id, name: v.inst.name, value: v.unit }))
 2808 |         });
 2809 |       }
 2810 |     });
 2811 | 
 2812 |     // Other (non-variable) fields: flag differing values across the compared spectra.
 2813 |     const others = SSNMR_EXPERIMENTAL_FIELDS.filter((f) => !effXFields.includes(f.key));
 2814 |     const norm = (raw) => { const n = parseManual(raw); return n !== null ? Math.round(n * 1e6) / 1e6 : String(raw).trim().toLowerCase(); };
 2815 |     others.forEach((f) => {
 2816 |       const valued = relevant
 2817 |         .map((inst) => ({ inst, raw: getExpValue(inst, f.key) }))
 2818 |         .filter((v) => v.raw !== '' && v.raw !== undefined && v.raw !== null);
 2819 |       if (valued.length < 2) return;
 2820 |       const counts = new Map();
 2821 |       valued.forEach((v) => { const k = norm(v.raw); counts.set(k, (counts.get(k) || 0) + 1); });
 2822 |       if (counts.size <= 1) return;
 2823 |       let refKey = null, refCount = -1;
 2824 |       counts.forEach((cnt, k) => { if (cnt > refCount) { refCount = cnt; refKey = k; } });
 2825 |       const refValue = valued.find((v) => norm(v.raw) === refKey)?.raw;
 2826 |       const offenders = valued.filter((v) => norm(v.raw) !== refKey).map((v) => ({ instId: v.inst.id, name: v.inst.name, value: v.raw }));
 2827 |       if (offenders.length) issues.push({ field: f, refValue, offenders });
 2828 |     });
 2829 |     return issues;
 2830 |   }, [series, effXFields.join('|'), d.instances, used]);
